个人简介
男声 本音青年音 全声线 从业 4 年

圈名 CV小蛟龙，男声配音演员，本音为青年音，音色可塑性强、全声线覆盖， 从业 4 年以来，擅长短剧、漫剧、动漫、广播剧、有声剧、游戏配音等多种类型的角色演绎。

参与的动漫作品有《憨豆先生》中文版、《咖宝车神之超能救援队》， 漫剧作品有《神级灵兽》《神战华夏》，游戏配音作品有《新三国》等多部作品。

}; const HEIGHTS = { expect: 121, normal: 120, happy: 125, curious: 136, satisfy: 124, sleep: 72 }; // ── Elements ───────────────────────────────────────────── const pet = document.getElementById('dragonPet'); const wrap = document.getElementById('dragonWrap'); const img = document.getElementById('dragonImg'); const bubble = document.getElementById('dragonBubble'); // Preload all sprites Object.values(SPRITES).forEach(src => { const i = new Image(); i.src = src; }); // ── State ───────────────────────────────────────────────── let x = window.innerWidth - 180; let y = window.innerHeight - 200; let tx = x, ty = y; let facing = -1; // 1=right, -1=left let state = 'idle'; let sleeping = false; let bubTimer, moveTimer; const SPEED = 1.5; const phrases = { idle: ['你好呀~', '嘿嘿♪', '今天去哪跑？', '(≧▽≦)', '要不要摸我？', '无聊…', '要陪我玩吗？'], click: ['唔！', '干嘛啦~', '嘿嘿嘿~', '好痒啊！', '(=^▽^=)', '摸我！', '哈哈~'], happy: ['耶！', '好开心！', '嘿嘿！', '太棒了！'], sleep: ['zzz…', '呼…', '好困…', '别吵我~'], satisfy: ['好吃！', '嗯嗯~', '满足♥'], }; // ── Helpers ─────────────────────────────────────────────── function setExpr(expr) { img.src = SPRITES[expr]; // Adjust height for sleep (landscape) img.style.height = HEIGHTS[expr] + 'px'; } function setState(s) { state = s; pet.className = 's-' + s; } function showBubble(text, ms = 2800) { bubble.textContent = text; bubble.classList.add('show'); clearTimeout(bubTimer); bubTimer = setTimeout(() => bubble.classList.remove('show'), ms); } function rand(arr) { return arr[Math.floor(Math.random() * arr.length)]; } function applyPos() { pet.style.left = x + 'px'; pet.style.top = y + 'px'; // Flip dragon wrap.style.transform = `scaleX(${facing})`; // Bubble always stays readable (not flipped) bubble.style.transform = 'translateX(-50%)'; } // ── Movement ────────────────────────────────────────────── function pickTarget() { const pad = 160; tx = pad + Math.random() * (window.innerWidth - pad * 2); ty = 60 + Math.random() * (window.innerHeight - 230); } function scheduleMove() { clearTimeout(moveTimer); if (sleeping) return; moveTimer = setTimeout(() => { pickTarget(); setState('walk'); setExpr('normal'); }, 2500 + Math.random() * 4500); } function maybeSleep() { if (Math.random() < 0.2) { sleeping = true; setState('sleep'); setExpr('sleep'); showBubble(rand(phrases.sleep), 99999); setTimeout(() => { sleeping = false; bubble.classList.remove('show'); setState('idle'); setExpr('normal'); scheduleMove(); }, 7000 + Math.random() * 8000); } else { scheduleMove(); } } // ── Idle speech ─────────────────────────────────────────── function scheduleIdleSpeech() { setTimeout(() => { if (state === 'idle' && !sleeping) { // Occasionally show satisfy or expect expression const r = Math.random(); if (r < 0.3) { setExpr('satisfy'); showBubble(rand(phrases.satisfy)); setTimeout(() => setExpr('normal'), 2800); } else if (r < 0.5) { setExpr('expect'); showBubble(rand(phrases.idle)); setTimeout(() => setExpr('normal'), 2800); } else { showBubble(rand(phrases.idle)); } } scheduleIdleSpeech(); }, 10000 + Math.random() * 14000); } // ── Click interaction ───────────────────────────────────── wrap.addEventListener('click', () => { if (sleeping) { sleeping = false; bubble.classList.remove('show'); setState('idle'); setExpr('normal'); showBubble('唔……被吵醒了…'); scheduleMove(); return; } clearTimeout(moveTimer); setState('happy'); setExpr('happy'); showBubble(rand(phrases.click), 2000); setTimeout(() => { setState('idle'); setExpr('normal'); scheduleMove(); }, 2200); }); // ── Animation loop ──────────────────────────────────────── function loop() { if (state === 'walk') { const dx = tx - x, dy = ty - y; const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy); if (d < 4) { x = tx; y = ty; setState('idle'); setExpr('normal'); // Occasionally show curious expression after arriving if (Math.random() < 0.3) { setExpr('curious'); setTimeout(() => setExpr('normal'), 2000); } maybeSleep(); } else { x += (dx / d) * SPEED; y += (dy / d) * SPEED; facing = dx > 0 ? 1 : -1; } } // Clamp to viewport x = Math.max(10, Math.min(window.innerWidth - 150, x)); y = Math.max(50, Math.min(window.innerHeight - 180, y)); applyPos(); requestAnimationFrame(loop); } // ── Init ────────────────────────────────────────────────── pet.style.position = 'fixed'; pet.style.left = x + 'px'; pet.style.top = y + 'px'; setExpr('normal'); setState('idle'); scheduleMove(); scheduleIdleSpeech(); loop(); })();
